    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/report.md\", \"content\": \"# Handover — 2026-05-20\\n\\n> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\\n\\n## 运行中\\n| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\\n|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\\n| exp_e_theta_adaptive | Threshold-Adaptive SC: N inverse scaling (D191) proves θ choice determines scaling direction. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: Structurally diverse prompt templates (role framing, output format variants, few-shot exemplar diversity) increase inter-sample entity extraction diversity beyond temperature-only diversity, improving construction F1 over identical-prompt N=8 sampling on FewNERD/SciERC/CoNLL.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_asb_adaptive_budget: Adaptive Sample Budget: extension of E_θ threshold optimization to sample count N. Instance features (degeneracy, LP variance, agreement rate) predict optimal N per instance. Under fixed total budget=8 (average), adaptive allocation outperforms uniform N=8 by allocating more samples to high-uncertainty instances and fewer to degenerate/easy ones.（排队等待 GPU）\\n- exp_lsc_latent_sc: Latent Self-Consistency: F4 diagnostic proves LP is compressed (median range 0.041 nats). Hidden states contain richer per-sample information than scalar LP. Entity-level mean-pooled hidden state representations enable latent aggregation (centroid selection, latent confidence scoring, clustering) that bypasses LP compression and provides discriminative per-sample ranking unavailable from output-space signals.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:2，等待提交）\\n- exp_mrsc_multi_round: Multi-Round Iterative SC: N inverse scaling reveals disputed entities cause failures. Focused re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement < 0.6) with weighted aggregation (Round 2 weight=2) should reduce error rate on disputed entities without affecting non-disputed ones, closing part of the selection gap through iterative refinement rather than single-round aggregation.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:3，等待提交）\\n- exp_esj_entity_self_judgment: Entity-Level Self-Judgment: F4 diagnostic proves 4/5 signals are instance-level only, LP is per-sample but compressed. ESJ provides true per-sample-per-entity signal via yes/no verification prompts. ESJ-weighted entity construction should outperform majority vote by leveraging entity-level confidence scores that structural signals cannot provide. Parent: exp_e2_mixture_of_signals (F4 LP-only diagnostic).（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:1，等待提交）\\n- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\\n- …另有 9 项\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp_14b_inference_scierc** (killed): 14B Qwen2.5 inference on SciERC (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): correlation-selection gap pattern should be consistent at 14B scale with intermediate degeneracy rates between 7B and 32B.\\n- **exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd** (success): The correlation-selection gap pattern is consistent at 14B scale, filling the gap between 8B and 32B. Qwen2.5-14B QLoRA fine-tuned on FewNERD should show intermediate degeneracy rates and similar signal ranking patterns, providing a smooth scaling curve for the diagnostic findings.\\n- **exp_b1_scd_conll_control** (negative): Negative control: SCD on CoNLL where construction gain is negative (-0.4pp, high degeneracy 70%). If SCD learns from construction pseudo-labels, it should NOT improve greedy F1 on a dataset where construction itself hurts. Expected: no improvement or slight degradation. Validates SCD mechanism.\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 103 / 负向 37 / 失败 4 / 共 173\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\n\\n## 告警\\n- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 7267, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline. | — | — | — |\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/report.md-9-| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/report.md-10-| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | —
